Conclusion: gpt-4o-mini is the better judge

The two judges agree on 99.4% of samples (789/794). Of the 5 disagreements, gpt-4o-mini is correct in 4 cases and gpt-5-nano in 1. The key pattern: nano has a false-negative bias — it marks answers as incorrect even when the predicted response clearly contains the right answer (ids 2356, 3793). Mini is better at extracting the core answer from verbose predictions. The one case where nano wins (id=2161): mini misclassified a wrong-but-attempted answer as not_attempted.

Question
What are the key features of the Yamaha YM2203, including the number of operator cells and their functionality in sound synthesis?
Gold Answer
The Yamaha YM2203 features 12 operator cells that generate sound via frequency-modulated digital sine waves. It has three concurrent FM synthesis channels, four operators per channel, and allows for independent frequency settings for channel three. Additionally, it includes two interval timers and implements Yamaha's YM2149F SSG chip.

Question
What were the circumstances surrounding the debut of the Canadian reality series *To Serve and Protect* on KVOS-TV, and what impact did it have on police departments featured in the show?
Gold Answer
The Canadian reality series *To Serve and Protect* debuted on KVOS-TV in 1993, created by Dwayne Mitchell and Dan Forrer. It featured police ride-alongs primarily in the Vancouver region. The show faced backlash from the Vancouver Police Department, which refused to allow further ride-alongs after the production company declined to blur the faces of suspects. This led to a cancellation of the original version in 1995 due to concerns over how police tactics were portrayed, prompting the RCMP of Surrey to suspend participation as well.

Question
What are the details about the genus Oxyruncus, including its origin, the naturalist who described the sharpbill, and the four subspecies of the sharpbill?
Gold Answer
The genus Oxyruncus was established by Dutch zoologist Coenraad Jacob Temminck in 1820. The sharpbill was first described by English naturalist William John Swainson in 1821 under the name Oxyrhuncus cristatus. The sharpbill has four subspecies: Oxyruncus cristatus frater (Costa Rica and west Panama), Oxyruncus cristatus brooksi (east Panama), Oxyruncus cristatus hypoglaucus (southeast Venezuela, the Guianas, and north Brazil), and Oxyruncus cristatus cristatus (southeast Brazil, east Paraguay, and northeast Argentina).
Model Prediction
The genus Oxyruncus was established by Dutch zoologist Coenraad Jacob Temminck in 1820. The sharpbill was first described by English naturalist William John Swainson in 1821 under the name Oxyrhuncus cristatus. The sharpbill has four subspecies: Oxyruncus cristatus frater (Costa Rica and west Panama), Oxyruncus cristatus brooksi (east Panama), Oxyruncus cristatus hypoglaucus (southeast Venezuela, the Guianas, and north Brazil), and Oxyruncus cristatus cristatus (southeast Brazil, east Paraguay, and northeast Argentina).

What are some key details about Davyd Whaley's life, including his birthplace, career, marriage, and contributions to art and philanthropy?
Gold Answer
Davyd Whaley was born in Bristol, Tennessee, in 1967. He was a Los Angeles-based painter and a United States Navy Veteran. Before becoming a painter, he worked as an electrical engineer for twenty years. Whaley was primarily self-taught but studied at the Art Students League of New York. He was known for his vibrant painting style and Jungian themes. What are the key details about Chanie Rosenberg's life, including her birth date, family background, political affiliations, and contributions as an artist and writer?
Gold Answer
Chanie Rosenberg was born on 20 April 1922 to a Jewish Zionist family originally from Lithuania in South Africa. She was the sister of poet Isaac Rosenberg and became an anti-Zionist revolutionary socialist after moving to Palestine in 1944. She later moved to Britain, where she was a member of the Revolutionary Communist Party and a founder of the Socialist Workers Party. In addition to her political activism, she was an artist whose sculptures were exhibited at the Royal Academy of Arts and authored several writings on education and socialism.

What is the history and significance of the Francis P. Garvan–John M. Olin Medal, including its designer and the criteria for nomination?
Gold Answer
The Francis P. Garvan–John M. Olin Medal was designed by Margaret Christian Grigor and established in 1936 by Francis Garvan and Mabel Brady Garvan to honor their daughter. Question
What are the key features of Apharwat Peak, including its elevation, location, and accessibility for skiing and snowboarding?
Gold Answer
Apharwat Peak is situated at an elevation of 14,403 feet (4,390 metres) in Gulmarg, India. It receives heavy snowfall and remains snow-covered for much of the year. The peak is accessible via a gondola that takes skiers and snowboarders to heights between 3,900 and 4,100 metres. The area is also located just a few kilometres from the Line of Control (LOC) and is dependent on weather conditions for access.

Question
What are the key contributions and achievements of Gang Chen in the field of mechanical engineering, including his educational background and significant discoveries?
Gold Answer
Gang Chen received his Doctor of Philosophy in mechanical engineering from the University of California, Berkeley in 1993, with a thesis titled 'Microscale thermal phenomena in optical and optoelectronic thin film devices.' He was an assistant professor at Duke University from 1993 to 1997 and later a tenured associate professor at UCLA from 1996 to 2001 before joining MIT in 2001. His major contributions include advancements in thermoelectricity, nanotechnology, and thermal engineering. Notably, in 2022, he and his colleagues discovered that cubic boron arsenide is a highly effective semiconductor. He has received numerous awards, including the NSF Young Investigator Award and election to the National Academy of Engineering in 2010.

Question
What are the geographical features, maximum depth, and significant islands of the Aegean Sea, including its length in miles?
Gold Answer
The Aegean Sea is an elongated embayment of the Mediterranean Sea located between Europe and Asia, covering an area of approximately 215,000 km² (83,000 sq mi). It reaches a maximum depth of 2,639 m (8,658 ft) to the west of Karpathos and has a length of about 430 miles. Significant islands include Crete, Rhodes, and those in the Dodecanese, Cyclades, Sporades, Saronic islands, and North Aegean Islands.

Significant islands include Crete, Rhodes, and those in the Dodecanese, Cyclades, Sporades, Saronic islands, and North Aegean Islands.

What were the significant roles and contributions of Bakshi Ghulam Mohammed in the political landscape of Kashmir, and how did his actions during the freedom struggle lead to him earning the title 'Khalid-e-Kashmir'?
Gold Answer
Bakshi Ghulam Mohammed was a prominent Kashmiri politician who displayed exceptional organizational skills and hard work during the freedom struggle, earning him the title 'Khalid-e-Kashmir'. He organized students and workers, set up unions, and was imprisoned multiple times, including a sixteen-month term in Reasi sub-Jail. After the 'Quit Kashmir' movement, he became Deputy Prime Minister and later Prime Minister of the State, as well as President of the National Conference. He is remembered as the 'Architect of Modern Kashmir' for his constructive work and ability to connect with people, setting Kashmir on a path to progress and embodying the ideal of 'Naya Kashmir'.

He is remembered as the 'Architect of Modern Kashmir' for his constructive work and ability to connect with people, setting Kashmir on a path to progress and embodying the ideal of 'Naya Kashmir'.

Jung Bahadur Rana, originally named Bir Narsingh Kunwar, was the 1st Maharaja of Lamjung and Kaski and served as the Prime Minister of Nepal. He eliminated factional fighting at court, removed rivals like the Pandes and Basnyats, introduced bureaucratic and judicial innovations, and modernized Nepal. However, he is also criticized for initiating a dark period marked by tyranny, economic exploitation, and religious persecution.

Question
What are the key details about the asteroid 83 Beatrix, including its discovery, size estimates, and the significance of its name?
Gold Answer
83 Beatrix is a large asteroid discovered by Annibale de Gasparis on April 26, 1865, which was his last asteroid discovery. It has a diameter of at least 68 kilometers, determined from a stellar occultation in 1983, and was later estimated to be 81.4 km from previous observations. The asteroid is named after Beatrice Portinari, who was beloved by Dante Alighieri and featured in his works La Vita Nuova and The Divine Comedy.
Model Prediction
83 Beatrix is a large asteroid discovered by Annibale de Gasparis on April 26, 1865, which was his last asteroid discovery. It has a diameter of at least 68 kilometers, determined from a stellar occultation in 1983, and was later estimated to be 81.4 km from previous observations. The asteroid is named after Beatrice Portinari, who was beloved by Dante Alighieri and featured in his works La Vita Nuova and The Divine Comedy.

What are the qualification criteria and the countries that secured spots for the women's team épée event at the 2020 Tokyo Olympics, including the 8th place finisher?
Gold Answer
The qualification for the women's team épée event at the 2020 Tokyo Olympics included 8 dedicated quota spots allocated based on the world team ranking list of April 5, 2021. The top 4 spots went to China, Poland, ROC, and South Korea. The next four spots were allocated to separate geographic zones, which included Hong Kong (Asia/Oceania), the United States (Americas), and Italy (Europe), with Estonia receiving the last spot due to no African team being in the top 16. The ROC finished in 8th place in the women's épée team event.

Gold Answer
Mitsuaki Tanigawa, known as the Ninja of Heisei, conducted over 254 break-ins in Osaka from March 2009 to June 2017, causing approximately ¥30,000,000 in damages. He was arrested in July 2017 at the age of 74 after being caught on surveillance removing his face covering. Tanigawa admitted to his crimes, stating he hated working and believed stealing was quicker. His meticulous planning and ability to navigate tight spaces while dressed as a ninja contributed to his notoriety.

Question
What significant events and changes occurred in the history of the Computer History Museum from its inception in 1968 until its reopening in 2011?
Gold Answer
The Computer History Museum originated in 1968 when Gordon Bell sought to create a historical collection. Its first exhibit was held in 1975 in a converted coat closet. In 1978, it became The Digital Computer Museum and opened to the public in September 1979. It incorporated as The Computer Museum in 1982 and moved to Boston in 1984. In 1996/1997, the TCM History Center was established at Moffett Field with artifacts shipped from TCM. TCM ceased operations in 1999, and the name was changed to Computer History Museum in 2000. CHM opened its new building in Mountain View, California, in 2002, and underwent a $19 million renovation, reopening in January 2011.
Model Prediction
The Computer History Museum originated in 1968 when Gordon Bell sought to create a historical collection. Its first exhibit was held in 1975 in a converted coat closet. In 1978, it became The Digital Computer Museum and opened to the public in September 1979. It incorporated as The Computer Museum in 1982 and moved to Boston in 1984. In 1996/1997, the TCM History Center was established at Moffett Field with artifacts shipped from TCM. TCM ceased operations in 1999, and the name was changed to Computer History Museum in 2000. CHM opened its new building in Mountain View, California, in 2002, and underwent a $19 million renovation, reopening in January 2011.

Question
What significant artworks did Claribel and Etta Cone acquire from Henri Matisse and other artists, and in which years were these acquisitions made, particularly focusing on the purchase of 'Blue Nude'?
Gold Answer
The Cones acquired several significant artworks from Henri Matisse, including 'The Music Lesson' and 'Two Women Seated on a Divan' in 1921, 'Standing Odalisque Reflected in a Mirror' in 1922, and 'Blue Nude' (1907) in 1926 at the sale of John Quinn’s collection in Paris. They also purchased works by artists such as Paul Cézanne, Gustave Courbet, and Vincent van Gogh.

Question
What were the different height measurements for Mount Everest obtained by various surveys from 1975 to 1999, and how did Nepal respond to these findings?
Gold Answer
The Chinese survey in 1975 reported a height of 29,029.24 feet (8,848.11 meters). An Italian survey in 1987 found a height of 29,108 feet (8,872 meters). In 1999, an American survey using GPS determined the height to be 29,035 feet (8,850 meters), which was accepted by the National Geographic Society and specialists in geodesy and cartography. However, Nepal continued to use the 8,848 meters height established in 1954 and expressed a desire to conduct its own measurements.

Question
What are the details regarding the two busts of Pope Paul V created by Gian Lorenzo Bernini, including their locations, creation years, and any notable features or history?
Gold Answer
Gian Lorenzo Bernini created two busts of Pope Paul V. The first bust, made in 1618, is located in the Borghese Gallery in Rome. The second bust, created in 1621 shortly after the Pope's death, was commissioned by Cardinal Scipione Borghese and is now in the J. Paul Getty Museum in Los Angeles. Additionally, there is a bronze version of the sculpture in the Statens Museum for Kunst in Copenhagen, Denmark. The second bust was considered lost for a long time until it reappeared at an auction in Slovakia in 2014.

Question
What are the details regarding the first typhoon of the 2019 Pacific typhoon season, including its name, classification, and significant meteorological events during its development?
Gold Answer
The first typhoon of the 2019 Pacific typhoon season is called Wutip. It originated from a low-pressure area on February 16, 2019, was classified as a tropical depression on February 18, and became a tropical storm the next day. Wutip underwent rapid intensification, reaching peak winds of 270 km/h (165 mph) and becoming the first Category 5-equivalent super typhoon on record in February. It caused significant damage in the Federated States of Micronesia and Guam before dissipating over the Philippine Sea on March 2.

What were the significant achievements and adventures of Frank Schreider during his lifetime, including details about his travels, books, and the circumstances of his death?
Gold Answer
Frank Schreider, who died on January 21, 1994, at the age of 70 from a heart attack aboard his sloop Sassafras off Crete, was an intrepid explorer known for circumnavigating the globe and retracing ancient seamen's routes. He and his wife, Helen, sailed from the Arctic Circle to Tierra del Fuego, walked and canoed the entire 4,000 miles of the Amazon, and created a floating jeep called 'La Tortuga' for their travels. Question
What are the characteristics and historical significance of the ukpe-okhue crown traditionally worn by the Iyoba (Queen Mother) of the Oba of Benin, including its origin and design features?
Gold Answer
The ukpe-okhue, meaning 'parrot's beak' in Edo, is a crown traditionally worn by the Iyoba (Queen Mother) of the Oba of Benin. It is made of a snood-like net of precious coral beadwork using ileke ('royal') cylindrical beads. This crown originated with the first queen mother, Idia, who was the first woman allowed to wear ileke beads. Its design resembles a pointed hat similar to a Phrygian cap, with the point curving forward.
Model Prediction
The ukpe-okhue, meaning 'parrot's beak' in Edo, is a crown traditionally worn by the Iyoba (Queen Mother) of the Oba of Benin. It is made of a snood-like net of precious coral beadwork using ileke ('royal') cylindrical beads. This crown originated with the first queen mother, Idia, who was the first woman allowed to wear ileke beads. Its design resembles a pointed hat similar to a Phrygian cap, with the point curving forward.

Question
What were the circumstances surrounding the formation of the Samajwadi Janata Party, including its founders, the date it was formed, and its initial political support?
Gold Answer
The Samajwadi Janata Party (SJP) was formed on 5 November 1990 when Shekhar and Devi Lal broke away from the governing Janata Dal. The party was able to gather 60 MPs and form a government with support from the Indian National Congress (Indira), with Shekhar serving as prime minister. The SJP government lasted for seven months, from 10 November 1990 to 21 June 1991, before collapsing when the INC(I) withdrew support.

The SJP government lasted for seven months, from 10 November 1990 to 21 June 1991, before collapsing when the INC(I) withdrew support.

What were the details of the 2022 Uttarakhand avalanche, including the date, location, number of deaths, and the response from the Nehru Institute of Mountaineering?
Gold Answer
The 2022 Uttarakhand avalanche occurred on 4 October 2022 at the Draupadi Ka Danda peak in Uttarkashi, India, resulting in the deaths of 27 mountaineers, including 24 trainees and 2 instructors. Question
What are the key geographical and ecological features of Manasbal Lake, including its depth, surrounding villages, and significance for local wildlife?
Gold Answer
Manasbal Lake, located in Ganderbal district of Jammu and Kashmir, India, has an average depth of 4.5 m (15 ft) and a maximum depth of 13 m (43 ft). It is surrounded by four villages: Jarokbal, Kondabal, Nesbal, and Gratbal. The lake is renowned for its lotus blooms, serves as a significant spawning ground for aquatic birds, and is often referred to as the 'supreme gem of all Kashmir Lakes.'

It is surrounded by four villages: Jarokbal, Kondabal, Nesbal, and Gratbal. The lake is renowned for its lotus blooms, serves as a significant spawning ground for aquatic birds, and is often referred to as the 'supreme gem of all Kashmir Lakes.'

Question
What is Rafoogar baithak, and how does it relate to the traditional craft of Rafoogari?
Gold Answer
Rafoogar baithak is an initiative aimed at supporting and preserving the dying craft of Rafoogari, which involves the traditional art of mending torn clothing and repairing fabric. This initiative seeks to promote the skills of rafoogars, who are artisans specializing in making damaged clothes almost invisible.

The Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Novo Theatre, designed by architect Ali Imam, serves as a non-formal science educational institute aimed at eradicating superstitions and promoting scientific temper, especially among students. Question
What are the key historical milestones in the establishment and development of the municipality of Segovia, Antioquia, Colombia, including its founding date and significant events that followed?
Gold Answer
Segovia was founded on July 24, 1869. It became a corregimiento in 1877, was established as a district in 1880, and on September 17, 1884, it was officially recognized as a district by the president of the state of Antioquia, Liciano Restrepo. The first mayor, Alejandro Yepes, was appointed shortly after its recognition.

The first mayor, Alejandro Yepes, was appointed shortly after its recognition.

What were the key events in Limpho Hani's political career, including her elections, resignations, and roles within the ANC from 1994 to 1999?

Question
What details can you provide about Agostina Pietrantoni's early life, including her birthplace, family background, and significant events leading to her religious vocation?
Gold Answer
Agostina Pietrantoni was born Livia Pietrantoni on 27 March 1864 in Pozzaglia Sabina, about 50 kilometers north-east of Rome, as the second of eleven children to poor farmers Francesco Pietrantoni and Caterina Costantini. She made her First Communion in 1868 and received her Confirmation in 1876. Starting work in 1871, she engaged in manual labor and later traveled to Tivoli for the olive harvest. In January 1886, she moved to Rome with her priest uncle to pursue her religious vocation, eventually joining the Sisters of Divine Charity and taking the name Agostina.
Model Prediction
Agostina Pietrantoni was born Livia Pietrantoni on 27 March 1864 in Pozzaglia Sabina, about 50 kilometers north-east of Rome, as the second of eleven children to poor farmers Francesco Pietrantoni and Caterina Costantini. She made her First Communion in 1868 and received her Confirmation in 1876. Starting work in 1871, she engaged in manual labor and later traveled to Tivoli for the olive harvest. In January 1886, she moved to Rome with her priest uncle to pursue her religious vocation, eventually joining the Sisters of Divine Charity and taking the name Agostina.

What was the purpose of the small vacant triangle of land bought by the Victoria and Albert Museum in 1863, and what significant features are associated with the Museum's main entrance?

The small vacant triangle of land opposite the main entrance was bought by the Museum in 1863 to ensure that the view from Thurloe Square could never be obscured by new buildings. What significant role did Seiko play during the 18th Olympiad in Tokyo in 1964, and what technological advancements did they introduce that year?

What are the key details about the municipality of San Roque, Antioquia, including its founding date, founder, geographical location, and population as of 2015?
Gold Answer
San Roque, Antioquia, was founded on 8 February 1880 by Francisco Martinez de Ospina. It is located in the Northeastern subregion of Antioquia, Colombia, at an elevation of 1,475 m (4,839 ft) above sea level, between the Nare River and the Nus River. Question
What significant events occurred in the history of Heliconia, Antioquia, Colombia, leading up to its foundation in 1814, and what was its original name before it was officially recognized?
Gold Answer
Heliconia was originally known as Mungia or Murgia and later referred to as the Pueblo de la Sal due to the indigenous salt production. The foundation of the population was decreed by Dictator Don Juan del Corral in 1814, although it was not officially recognized as a municipality until 1831. The area was rich in indigenous guacas and salinas, which attracted Spanish explorers in the 16th century.

Kiyosi Ito collaborated with Yukiyosi Kawada to publish 'On the Probability Distribution on a Compact Group' in 1940.
